- CountWording (Models/CountWording.swift): a count with the right singular or plural word, so the screen and VoiceOver say the same thing (hours(_:), minutes(_:), and seconds(_:), as in "1 minute", which TimeFormatting.spoken's durations use too, and part(_:of:singular:plural:), as in "0 of 1 book", the noun agreeing with the whole), a time kept in tenths of a second (secondsToTheTenth(_:), "0.5 seconds" or "2 seconds", for the gesture time window and the double-press time), how far a batch has got, as in "3 of 10 books processed" and "0 of 1 book processed" (part(_:of:_:), with the CountWording.Noun a progress overlay counts by, ProgressOverlay.itemNoun: ImportProgressWording.itemNoun and TextExtractionProgress.itemNoun, REV13), and the library's books with its feeds beside them, as in "743 books, 2 feeds" (booksAndFeeds(books:feeds:), FR11), which every count of the library's books uses; where episodes and articles are listed beside books, as in Recently Opened and the Reading List, each kind is counted apart, "9 books, 1 article" (listItems(books:episodes:articles:feeds:) and listItems(of:feeds:), of which booksAndFeeds is the case without feed items), and a section is headed the same way, "Books (9), Articles (1)" (booksHeader(of:)). CountWording.list(_:) is the one "a, b, and c" joiner, which the cloud, removal, feed, shortcut, and filter wording use.
- ManualBookOrder and LibraryBookSorter (ViewModels/LibraryViewModel+SortAndOrder.swift, Models/LibraryBookSorter.swift): the library's orderings. Manual order (by position, then title, then book ID) is the one order a folder's list, Move Book Up and Down, dragging, and Continue to Next Book all follow; the reading list keeps an order of its own (ReadingList), which Move Book Up and Down and dragging rearrange at the Reading List through the same LibraryReorderTarget.
- RemovalWording, FeedDownloadRemovalWording, OperationOutcome, ReadingStatsTileWording, ImportDecisionWording, BookExportWording, BookTextEditWording, TranscriptionWording, TranscriptCleanupWording, AudioEditWording, ReadingListWording, ReadingListReaderWording, EndOfBookWording, ReaderTimeWording, ImportProgressWording, FeedDownloadProgressWording, and SpeechRefusalWording (Models/, FeedDownloadRemovalWording in RemovalWording.swift and the last in SpeechRefusalReport.swift): the wording of removals, the Remove of a feed's downloads, operation results, statistics, import questions, Export, Edit Text, transcription, the transcript cleanup, audio edits, the reading list, a book read from it, the end of a book, with the one word, "Finished" (EndOfBookWording.finishedProgress, P07), that the library row, Speak Info, Where Am I?, and Copy Reading Position say of a book resting at its end, while the reader's status row keeps its percent and time, the reader's times (its jump fields' labels, refusals, and announcements, the copied place, and the speed named beside time left and total), an import's progress, the feed downloads' progress, which says its sizes, its time left, and its lines to VoiceOver as an import's progress does (ImportProgressWording.spokenStatus(title:lines:)), and the report of a speech refusal, each built once for both the screen and VoiceOver.
- LibraryInfoWording (Models/LibraryInfoWording.swift): every sentence Speak Info says of a book, a folder, or a feed, brief or full, made from facts alone (BookInfoFacts, FolderInfoFacts, and FeedInfoFacts) and pinned by LibraryInfoWordingTests without a window: counts through CountWording, times through TimeFormatting, dates through FeedWording.moment, and a feed's counts through its row's own phrase, FeedWording.countsPhrase.
- LibraryCloudWording (Models/LibraryCloudWording.swift), ReaderCloudWording (ViewModels/ReaderViewModel+CloudDownload.swift), DataDirectoryUnavailableText (Views/Library/DataDirectoryUnavailableView.swift), RecordingWording (Views/Recording/RecordingWording.swift), AudioEditorWording (Views/AudioEditor/AudioEditorWording.swift), and BookVoiceSettingsWording (Views/Reader/PlaybackControlsView.swift): the wording of the list's cloud commands, of the refusal of books only in the cloud by operations that never download (LibraryCloudWording.downloadFirst(count:) and notDownloaded(titles:), which a move of the library, Export, and Edit Text share), of a reader's download, the Library Not Available screen, the Record window, the audio editor window, and saving and resetting a book's voice, speed, and pitch, which the playback bar and the voice sheet share, each built once for both the screen and VoiceOver.
- FeedWording (Views/Feeds/FeedWording.swift and the files beside it): every word of the feed reader's interface, its rows, sheets, commands, notifications, and announcements, each label made by a function so the hosted tests can pin it.
- FeedItemPackage.removeWhole(_:in:remove:) (Services/Feeds/FeedItemPackage.swift): the one whole-package removal; a package already gone counts as removed.
- Book.isUnopened (Models/Book.swift) and Library.booksInFolderTree(_:) (Models/Library.swift) are the one definition of "never opened" and of "a folder's tree of books" (the library's own books in the folder and below it, never a feed's items): Book.isUnopened is read by the Unopened category, a row's New badge, Speak Info, a feed item's New and its kept package's test, and the recently opened sort; booksInFolderTree by a folder's emptiness test (hasBooks(inFolderTree:), which stops at the first book), its book counts, and its Speak Info. Delete Folder… lists the books it removes with booksInFolderTree, and Library.removeFolderAndContents(id:) removes the same books, so a feed's item that names a folder is neither deleted nor dropped; Library.isPath(_:inTreeAt:) is the one "is this path the folder or below it" rule, by whole path components, which the folders, their sort settings, and the books all follow.
- Array's subscript(safe:) (TextMouthProductionTests/CollectionTestSupport.swift) is the tests' one way to read an element they have not proven is there, so a break in the code under test fails an assertion rather than trapping the test host.

## Known duplication

Repeated code that is known and left for now, each with the reason:

- More than a dozen messages still choose singular or plural inline, in FileImportService, LegacyBookMigration, LibraryViewModel+Migration, PersistenceFailureCenter, LibraryViewModel+BookOperations, and AudioBookParser; they could use CountWording. A few sentences deliberately drop the number for one ("Remove Book?", "the book inside") and do not fit it.
- TimeRemainingBar.computeTimeRemaining (Views/Library/LibrarySubviews.swift) repeats LibraryViewModel.totalTimeRemaining(books:wpm:) (ViewModels/LibraryViewModel+Migration.swift), which nothing calls at all, applying each book's live reading position first.
- PDFTextParser's two page readers, with and without heading detection, share most of their loop, but record font sizes differently in ways that the paragraph joiner reads, so merging them would change some PDFs' paragraphs.
- VoiceOverFocus.swift repeats its accessibility-tree searches (findElementByIdentifier and findFirstInteractiveElement) and its focus scheduling in StandardVoiceOverFocusTriggerView and VoiceOverFocusTriggerView.
- Several stores configure their own JSON encoder with the same ISO 8601 dates and sorted, pretty output (bookInfo.json in BookInfo, RemovedFromLibraryMarker, ReadingStatsService, DataDirectoryMigrator, LibraryIdentity, LibrarySessionLease, and AudioEditHistoryStore, which also leaves slashes unescaped), while library.json is written compact with its keys unsorted (LibraryPersistenceService.encodedIndex(_:)), and contentIndex.json and reading-checkpoints.json compact with sorted keys, so sharing one encoder would change those files' layouts. DataDirectoryMigrator writes a moved library's library.json with its own pretty, sorted encoder rather than encodedIndex(_:), so the index stays pretty-printed after a move until the library is next saved.
- ReaderViewModel.offMain repeats OffMainWork.run: both run blocking work on a global dispatch queue and hand back its result. Two cloud branches written side by side added them; the reader could call OffMainWork.
- Three functions compare two items' volume identifiers: ReaderWorkingCopies.isOnSameVolume, ImportScratchSpace.areOnSameVolume, which first climbs to the nearest folder that exists, and the live areOnOneVolume probe of StorageDestinationAssessment. They could share one.
- ListingFailure (Services/ReaderWorkingCopy.swift) and FolderListingFailure (Services/DataDirectoryMigrator.swift) are the same small class, keeping the first error a folder enumeration's handler meets; one could serve both.
- ContentView opens readers with the literal window ID "book-reader" rather than BookReaderWindowView.windowID, and posts its folder announcement directly rather than through VoiceOverAnnouncer.
- ContentFileWatcher (Services/BookTextEditor.swift) repeats ConfigService's file watcher: a DispatchSource on a descriptor opened for events only, armed again when a save replaces the file.
- TextMouthPaths.cachedAudioDir(in:) names a per-package cachedAudio folder (TextMouthPaths.cachedAudioFolderName, which a package's import leaves out) that nothing in the app creates or reads.
- The performance tests keep, verbatim, the code each change of October 3 replaced, as the oracle and the baseline the new code is compared with: each speech-preparation test file keeps its own privately (legacyTransform, legacyPauses, legacyRuns, legacyPreparedSpeech), and the text pipeline's are kept once, in TextPipelineBefore (TextMouthProductionTests/TextPipelineFixtures.swift), for the golden tests and the benchmarks. They are frozen on purpose and must not be changed to call the new code.
- FeedBadge (Views/Feeds/FeedRows.swift) draws the same capsule that BookRow (Views/Library/LibrarySubviews.swift) draws inline for New and Now Reading: white words on a colored background, in the same font and padding. The book row could use it.
- LibraryInfoSpeaker (ViewModels/LibraryInfoSpeaker.swift) repeats the utterance Where Am I? makes (speakWhereAmI, in ViewModels/ReaderViewModel+WhereAmI.swift: the text guarded, the voice, SpeechService.rateForWPM, and prefersAssistiveTechnologySettings off) and, since FR12's branch C, its handling of the end of one: finishes and cancels told apart by ReportedUtterance, a check that the one reported is the one speaking, and a synthesizer replaced when the engine refused it or a summary takes the place of one still speaking (its isLive(_:) and replaceSynthesizer() beside the reader's isLiveWhereAmIUtterance(_:) and replaceWhereAmISynthesizer()), its LibraryInfoSpeechDelegate doing what WhereAmIDelegateHelper (ViewModels/ReaderViewModel.swift) does. One speaker of short announcements in the app's own voice could serve both.
- ImportProgressWording.timeOnBook(_:) (Models/ImportProgressWording.swift) counts the time on a book in whole 5-second steps, as ReaderCloudWording.elapsed(since:at:) (ViewModels/ReaderViewModel+CloudDownload.swift) counts a download's wait, so VoiceOver and a braille display don't churn; one TimeFormatting helper could serve both (board #1460).
- FeedRecordAbsence's notesOfThisMac(in:) (ViewModels/LibraryViewModel+FeedDownloadReturn.swift) lists a folder without downloading anything, as FeedItemPackage.entryNames(of:) does; both could list through one helper.
- BookSettingsSheetWording (Views/Reader/ReaderView.swift), which names a book's own settings sheet and its toolbar button, and BookVoiceSettingsWording (Views/Reader/PlaybackControlsView.swift), which words saving and resetting the book's voice, speed, and pitch, are two wording types for the same per-book settings; A11 added the first without moving the second, and one type could hold both.
